## Methodology

### Extraction Strategies

1. **Pattern Matching:** Identified capitalized sequences with geographic indicators
2. **Geographic Features:** Extracted explicit patterns like 'Name + Type' (e.g., Victoria Island)
3. **Section Analysis:** Targeted sections like 'Principal Towns', 'Geographical Features'
4. **Table Extraction:** Extracted location names from population and administrative tables
5. **Context Validation:** Verified toponyms using surrounding context

### Sources Scanned

- Manual parsed files from `output_2/{year}_manual_parsed/`
- All colony markdown files per year

### Quality Assurance

- Filtered common words and invalid patterns
- Excluded government titles, month names, and directional words
- Deduplicated toponyms by normalized name
- Added full provenance with source file, line number, and context
- Confidence score: 0.95 for all extracted toponyms

## Output Files

Enhanced knowledge graph files with toponyms:

- `knowledge_graph_extracts_v3/1946_extracted_toponyms.json`
- `knowledge_graph_extracts_v3/1948_extracted_toponyms.json`
- `knowledge_graph_extracts_v3/1949_extracted_toponyms.json`

## Notes

- Years 1938, 1939, and 1940 were requested but do not have source data available
- Only years 1946, 1948, and 1949 were processed
- All toponyms include full provenance linking back to source documents
- Entity IDs follow format: `place_{year}_new_{###}`
